### [1] [Multistability and Juggling States in Frustrated Kuramoto Oscillators on Ring Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10818)
*Guilherme S. Costa,Ricardo Fariello,Marcus A. M. de Aguiar*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 本文研究环网络上矩阵耦合振荡器的性质，揭示多种同步聚类解族：交替序（含静态锁定簇与刚性旋转簇）和顺序序（受界面振荡器约束）。随机初相下系统倾向于形成动力学簇，其界面沿环运动、相遇时簇湮灭，直至界面同向运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望理解环拓扑下矩阵耦合振荡器系统的集体行为，特别是同步聚类结构的存在性、稳定性及其吸引域如何受模型参数影响，以刻画系统从随机初相到有序聚类态的自组织过程。

Method: 在环网络上构建矩阵耦合振荡器模型，解析推导两类解族：交替序（相邻振荡器属于不同簇，含静态锁定簇与刚性旋转簇）与顺序序解；分析簇数对邻居连通性的依赖及界面振荡器带来的平衡约束；并通过随机初相的数值模拟检验稳定性与吸引域，观察动力学簇的界面运动与湮灭。

Result: 系统支持多个同步聚类解族。交替序中，第一类解含一个或两个静态簇，其锁定相位依赖耦合参数（尽管自然频率非零）；第二类解为刚性旋转簇，簇数取决于邻居连通性。顺序序解受界面振荡器约束，对平衡构型施加额外限制。部分解虽稳定，但吸引域依赖模型参数；随机初相下振荡器自组织为动力学簇，界面沿环移动并在相遇时簇湮灭，最终达到界面同向运动的状态。

Conclusion: 环网络上矩阵耦合振荡器支持丰富的同步聚类解族，其结构与连通性、耦合参数密切相关。稳定解的吸引域受限，随机初相下系统自发形成界面运动的动力学簇并经历簇湮灭，最终收敛到界面同向传播的状态。

Abstract: We study the properties of matrix coupled oscillators in ring networks and show that the system supports several families of synchronized clustered solutions. One of these families is characterized by consecutive oscillators belonging to distinct clusters, called alternate ordering, with two classes of solutions. The first consists of one or two static clusters, that lock on phases that depend on the coupling parameters, despite the non-zero natural oscillation frequency. The second one is comprised of clusters that rotate rigidly. The number of clusters depends on the neighbor connectivity. We also investigated sequential ordered clusters and showed that the constraints imposed by the interface oscillators imposes additional restrictions to equilibrium configurations. Although some of these solutions are stable, their basins of attraction depend on model parameters: when the system is initialized with random phases, the oscillators tend to organize themselves into dynamical clusters whose interfaces move along the ring. The oscillators within each cluster remain close to one of the stable phase configurations, while particles are exchanged at the interfaces. When interfaces moving in opposite directions collide, the corresponding clusters annihilate, until a state with interfaces moving in a common direction is reached.

</details>


### [2] [A theory for detecting the Turing instability on static and temporal multiplex networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10938)
*Joshua Ritchie*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个在多重网络上进行线性不稳定性分析的通用框架，旨在检测无层间扩散情形下的图灵不稳定性，并将其应用于群岛上的两物种捕食-被捕食模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于建立一个能够检测多重网络（而非单一网络）上图灵不稳定性的通用理论框架，填补多重网络结构与图灵不稳定性交叉领域的方法空白。

Method: 方法上，针对含m个未知量、由非自治反应-扩散方程给出的解，在含n个节点的时序多重网络上建立完整的线性不稳定性分析形式化框架；并识别出一类拉普拉斯矩阵可交换的多重网络，在此条件下推导出不稳定性判据的显式不等式。

Result: 结果方面，框架以群岛上的两物种捕食-被捕食模型作为实例进行验证：节点对应岛屿，边对应各物种的运输网络，并利用非自治设定刻画多种自然现象。

Conclusion: 结论是所提框架具有通用性，在拉普拉斯矩阵可交换的网络子类中可得到解析性结果，并通过生态学实例展示了其在非自治、时序及多重结构下的适用性。

Abstract: The purpose of this work is to introduce and discuss a general framework for performing a linear instability analysis on multiplex networks, in the absence of inter-layer diffusion, with the specific motivation of detecting the Turing instability. The formalism itself is presented in full generality for $m$-unknowns, each of which arise as solutions of non-autonomous reaction-diffusion equations, on $n$-node temporal multiplex networks. In addition, we identify a class of multiplex networks for which the Laplacian matrices commute. In this setting analytical progress can be made and the explicit inequalities, governing instability, are derived. In order to explore our formalism we apply it to the specific example of a two-species predator-prey model on archipelagos. Here nodes are interpreted as islands and edges correspond to the transportation network for each species. Moreover, the non-autonomous setting is used to model a variety of natural phenomena.

</details>

### [3] [Kinetic Reduction and Hydrodynamics under Time-Dependent Helical Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10605)
*Qingyun Zeng,Fan Feng*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该文在目标分子动力学框架下建立了时间依赖螺旋对称稀薄气体的约化Boltzmann方程，并通过流体力学展开导出Euler与Navier-Stokes-Fourier方程，揭示了柱向几何与轴向形变对黏性输运、热传导及应力各向异性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平移对称流动已在动理学与连续介质框架中被广泛研究，但螺旋流动中微观动力学与连续介质行为之间的联系尚不完善，因此需要建立相应的理论桥梁。

Method: 采用目标分子动力学（objective molecular dynamics）框架，针对具有时间依赖螺旋对称性的稀薄气体，推导保留径向变化的约化Boltzmann方程，并保持经典碰撞算子在螺旋对称下的形式；随后通过形式化流体力学展开得到Euler方程与Navier-Stokes-Fourier方程。

Result: 所得模型刻画了圆柱几何与轴向形变对黏性输运和热传导的影响，以及形变与碰撞松弛竞争下沿轴与跨轴方向产生的不等应力。与直接模拟Monte Carlo结果的对比涵盖空间均匀与径向变化流动：一阶流体修正能捕捉主导应力各向异性并改善径向温度变化预测，而在较大Knudsen数下该近似存在局限。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了螺旋对称稀薄气体中微观动力学与连续介质描述之间的系统联系，验证了一阶流体修正的有效性，同时指出高Knudsen数下近似方法的适用范围边界。

Abstract: Translational and helical symmetries provide idealized descriptions of many flows encountered in nature and engineering. While translationally symmetric flows have been extensively studied within kinetic and continuum frameworks, the corresponding connection between microscopic dynamics and continuum behaviour for helical flows is less developed. Here, we investigate this connection for a dilute gas with time-dependent helical symmetry. Using the objective molecular dynamics framework, we derive a reduced Boltzmann equation that retains radial variations while preserving the classical collision operator under helical symmetry. Formal hydrodynamic expansions then yield the corresponding Euler and Navier-Stokes-Fourier equations. The resulting models describe how cylindrical geometry and axial deformation influence transport, including viscous transport and heat conduction, and how deformation generates unequal stresses along and across the axis in competition with collisional relaxation. Comparisons with direct simulation Monte Carlo solutions examine both spatially uniform and radially varying flows. In the cases studied, the first-order fluid corrections capture the leading stress anisotropy and improve the prediction of radial temperature variations, while larger Knudsen numbers expose limitations of the approximation.

</details>


### [4] [A critical-layer instability of structural acoustic wall modes in a two-flexible-plate channel with Poiseuille flow: closed-form low-Mach corrections and parity-protected absorption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10798)
*Ashray Saxena*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 研究两块薄弹性板间平面Poiseuille流的可压缩流体柱中结构-声壁面模态的耦合色散，报道了一种流动诱导的不稳定性：临界马赫数以上反对称模态不受临界层吸收，而对称模态呈对流不稳定性，并在无粘极限下增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 薄弹性壁承载平面Poiseuille流的流固耦合系统中，壁面模态与流动临界层的相互作用机制（尤其是无滑移条件下对流项消失带来的影响）尚不清晰，需要判定所观察到的失稳是粘性假象还是无粘临界层机制。

Method: 利用无滑移剖面上壁面对流（Ingard-Myers）项恒为零、板导纳条件与马赫数无关的性质，通过Fredholm可解性论证给出每个耦合波数的闭式O(M)修正；采用空间（锚定于耗散壁-斯托克斯项）与常规无关的时间两类计算确定增长率，并用Briggs-Bers分析进行分类。

Result: 存在临界马赫数Mc=Ω/ξ，超过后内部临界层首次出现，两支结构分支行为相反：反对称模态因中面压力节点（精确）而受保护、不被临界层吸收；对称模态对流不稳定，增长率在无粘极限下增强，属无粘临界层不稳定性而非粘性效应。中性曲线遵循Mc(Ω)，增长率正比于壁面柔度并在刚性壁时消失，故区别于Tollmien-Schlichting与Kelvin-Helmholtz机制。三种独立数值格式吻合到10^-9，闭式修正与三种剪切剖面的数值微分一致。

Conclusion: 该失稳是一种由临界层主导的无粘对流不稳定性，其存在依赖壁面柔度，机制上独立于经典Tollmien-Schlichting与Kelvin-Helmholtz不稳定，并可由闭式解析修正与多方法数值结果一致验证。

Abstract: A compressible fluid column between two thin elastic plates carrying plane Poiseuille flow supports discrete structural-acoustic wall modes. This paper analyses their coupled dispersion and reports a flow-induced instability. Because a no-slip profile has zero velocity at the wall, the convective (Ingard-Myers) term in the compliant-wall boundary condition vanishes identically, so the plate-admittance conditions stay Mach-independent and a Fredholm solvability argument yields a closed-form $O(M)$ correction to every coupled wavenumber, valid for any smooth no-slip shear profile. Above the critical Mach number $M_c=Ω/ξ$ at which an interior critical layer first appears, the two structural branches behave oppositely. The antisymmetric mode is not absorbed by the critical layer, a null protected by an exact mid-plane pressure node rather than by weak coupling. The symmetric mode, which carries no such node, is convectively unstable. Its growth rate, fixed both by a spatial calculation anchored to the dissipative wall-Stokes term and by a convention-independent temporal one, strengthens towards the inviscid limit, identifying an inviscid critical-layer instability rather than a viscous artefact. A Briggs-Bers analysis classifies it as convective. Its neutral curve follows $M_c(Ω)$, and its growth rate is proportional to the wall compliance, vanishing for rigid walls, so it is distinct from the Tollmien-Schlichting and Kelvin-Helmholtz mechanisms. Three independent numerical schemes agree to one part in $10^9$, and the closed-form correction matches numerical differentiation across three shear profiles.

</details>


### [5] [Geometric Mechanics of Real-Space Energy Transfer in Turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11029)
*Weijie Zhang,Hanxun Yao*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该论文建立了湍流能量级联与流体元局部变形/旋转动力学之间纯粹力学联系：在尺度ℓ的球面几何下，实空间能量级联可局部分解为应变自放大与涡旋拉伸，系数为球形流体邻域比转动惯量2/5(ℓ/2)²；数值模拟证实该贡献主导局部能量传递并界定正/逆级联事件的统计边界；纵横分解表明Kolmogorov 4/5律对应的纵向传递仅由应变自放大控制，涡旋拉伸仅通过横向分量进入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 湍流中跨尺度局部能量传递如何编码于流体元的瞬时变形，仍是一个基本未解问题。作者旨在从纯力学角度厘清能量级联与流体元局部旋转、变形动力学之间的具体联系，并区分纵向与横向传递的不同物理机制。

Method: 利用尺度ℓ处实空间能量级联的球面几何，进行精确推导，将局部能量传递分解为应变自放大与涡旋拉伸两项，其系数为球形流体邻域的比转动惯量2/5(ℓ/2)²；随后通过直接数值模拟（DNS）验证，并进一步作纵向与横向分解以区分两种机制的贡献。

Result: DNS表明该球面几何贡献捕获了主导的局部能量传递，并定义一个区分正级联与逆级联事件的统计边界；纵向与横向分解揭示Kolmogorov 4/5律所对应的纵向传递完全由应变自放大支配，而涡旋拉伸则通过横向分量进入。

Conclusion: 研究确立了湍流能量级联与流体元局部旋转、变形动力学之间纯粹的力学联系，为从流体元瞬时变形角度理解跨尺度能量传递提供了定量框架。

Abstract: How the local energy transfer across scales in turbulence is encoded in the instantaneous deformation of fluid elements remains a fundamental question. We exploit the spherical geometry of the real-space energy cascade at length scale $\ell$. An exact derivation shows that it can be decomposed locally by strain self-amplification and vortex stretching, with a coefficient equal to the specific moment of inertia $2/5(\ell/2)^2$ of a spherical fluid neighborhood. Direct numerical simulations show that this contribution captures the dominant local transfer and defines a statistical boundary separating forward- and inverse-cascade events. A further longitudinal and transverse decomposition reveals that the longitudinal transfer underlying Kolmogorov's $4/5$ law is governed solely by strain self-amplification, whereas vortex stretching enters through the transverse component. These results establish a purely mechanical connection between turbulent energy cascade and the local rotational and deformational dynamics of fluid elements.

</details>


### [6] [Inertialess instability of elasto-viscoplastic circular Couette flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11393)
*James D. Shemilt,Neil J. Balmforth,Duncan R. Hewitt*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 研究了Saramito（Bingham）本构弹黏塑性流体在二维圆Couette流中的稳定性：连续应力基态在零雷诺数下于低剪切率区域出现线性不稳定，失稳模态随方位波数增大而增强并局域于屈服面，非线性饱和后形成复杂时空状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弹黏塑性流体圆Couette流的基态存在非唯一性（应力依赖初始聚合物应力、屈服面处剪切率与应力可能不连续），需要厘清何种基态在零雷诺数下稳定，并刻画其失稳与非线性演化机制。

Method: 采用Saramito（Bingham）本构方程，构造轴对称基态并进行线性稳定性分析；通过数值模拟研究非线性动力学，并引入聚合物应力扩散以消除正应力不连续及最短波长处的不稳定。

Result: 具有连续应力剖面的基态在零雷诺数下，当包含相对低剪切率区域时呈线性不稳定；失稳模态随方位波数增大而增强并局域于屈服面；线性模态饱和为有限振幅，形成时空复杂状态；添加应力扩散后最短波长不稳定被消除。

Conclusion: 只要基流含有相对低剪切率区域，就会发生不稳定并向复杂动力学转变；基态非唯一性与屈服面处的间断性是该类流动稳定性的关键特征。

Abstract: An exploration is presented of two-dimensional, circular Couette flow of an elasto-viscoplastic fluid described by Saramito's (Bingham) constitutive law. Axisymmetrical base states are constructed. As has been noted previously, such states are not unique, with stresses over any plugs depending on the initial polymer stress, and the shear rate and stress may jump discontinuously across the yield surfaces. We show that base states with continuous stress profiles are linearly unstable at zero Reynolds number when they contain regions with relatively low shear rate. Unstable modes become stronger and localized to the yield surfaces as the azimuthal wavenumber increases. We report numerical simulations exploring the nonlinear dynamics of the instability. The linear unstable modes saturate at finite amplitude to create spatio-temporally complicated states. Polymer stress diffusion is added to ease these numerical simulations, eliminating normal-stress discontinuities and instability at the shortest wavelengths. Instability and transition to complex dynamics occur whenever the base flow contains regions with relatively low shear rate.

</details>


### [7] [Net drag reduction in turbulent pipe flow by airfoil-section grids concentrated near the wall](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11474)
*Takuya Masuda*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 采用NACA0006翼型格栅作为整流器，通过轴对称RANS数值模拟评估其在湍流管流中的净减阻效果，近壁同心环格栅可使流量提升最高9.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 湍流管流的摩擦阻力在水与天然气输送系统中造成巨大能耗；传统整流器虽能抑制湍流，但其引入的压降损失往往超过减阻收益，而管内大涡破碎装置也难以实现净减阻，因此亟需一种能实现净减阻的被动控制方案。

Method: 将NACA0006翼型构成的格栅用作整流器以抑制压降损失，采用轴对称RANS方程结合k-ω SST湍流模型与周期性边界条件进行数值模拟；模型先以充分发展湍流管流及既往管内大涡破碎装置实验进行验证，再评估格栅的净减阻性能。

Result: 在对应雷诺数约10^5的压差下，轴向间距0.5 m、集中于近壁区的同心环格栅使流量最高提升9.0%，且流量增益在5×10^4 ≤ Re ≤ 2×10^5范围内保持为正；该格栅使面积积分雷诺剪切应力降低29%，并使速度剖面更接近层流。

Conclusion: 近壁布置的有效性与Fukagata-Iwamoto-Kasagi恒等式一致，该恒等式对靠近壁面的雷诺剪切应力赋予更高权重，表明翼型格栅依靠近壁抑制湍流实现净减阻，具备实际应用潜力。

Abstract: The frictional drag of turbulent pipe flow is responsible for enormous energy consumption in water and natural gas distribution systems. Flow straighteners can reduce this drag by suppressing turbulence, but they generally induce a pressure loss that exceeds the reduction; similarly, large-eddy breakup devices installed in pipes have not achieved a net drag reduction. In this study, a grid of NACA0006 airfoils was adopted as a flow straightener to suppress the pressure loss, and its net drag reduction was evaluated by numerical simulations using the $k$-$ω$ shear stress transport model of the axisymmetric Reynolds-averaged Navier-Stokes equations with periodic boundary conditions. The model was validated against fully developed turbulent pipe flow and a previous experiment on a large-eddy breakup device in a pipe. Under a pressure difference corresponding to a Reynolds number ($\mathit{Re}$) of approximately $10^5$, a grid pattern of concentric rings concentrated near the wall at an axial spacing of 0.5 m increased the flow rate by up to 9.0%, and the flow rate increase remained positive for $5 \times 10^{4} \leq \mathit{Re} \leq 2 \times 10^{5}$. The grid pattern reduced the area-integrated Reynolds shear stress by 29% and brought the velocity profile closer to a laminar one. The effectiveness of the near-wall placement is consistent with the Fukagata-Iwamoto-Kasagi identity, which weights the Reynolds shear stress more heavily closer to the wall.

</details>


### [8] [Using analogues to investigate error growth and predictability in turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11796)
*Carlos Granero-Belinchon,Mickaël Bourgoin,Bérengère Dubrulle*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 通过实验湍流时间序列重新审视Lorenz的"类比"概念，识别出误差增长的三个 regime，并发现 Lyapunov 指数与代数 regime 指数依赖初始误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lorenz 最初提出"类比"（analogues）概念以研究误差增长与可预测性，但该概念在充分发展湍流中尚未通过实验数据得到系统验证；本文旨在以实验数据检验并量化该直觉。

Method: 将类比方法应用于一维湍流速度时间序列，追踪不同初始误差下的误差增长，并提取各阶段的增长指数。

Result: 成功识别出 Lorenz 直觉并由湍流理论形式化的三个误差增长 regime：混沌指数增长 regime、自发随机性的代数 regime、以及饱和 regime；发现 Lyapunov 指数与代数 regime 的指数均依赖初始误差。

Conclusion: 该依赖被解释为湍流间歇性导致的统计偏差——较大初始误差更偏向采样稀有事件（更低 Lyapunov 指数、更奇异的增量）；结果既验证了 Lorenz 的定性直觉，也为湍流可预测性及极端事件预测提供了定量新见解。

Abstract: In this study, we revisit Lorenz's pioneering concept of analogues to investigate error growth and predictability in fully developed turbulence using experimental data. By applying the method to a 1D turbulent velocity time series, we successfully identify the three error growth regimes intuited by Lorenz and later formalized by turbulence theory: the chaotic exponential regime, the algebraic regime of spontaneous stochasticity, and the saturation regime. Our analysis reveals that the Lyapunov exponent and the exponent of the algebraic regime depend on the initial error. We interpret this dependence as a statistical bias induced by the intermittency of turbulence, where rare events, characterized by lower Lyapunov exponents and more singular velocity increments, are preferentially sampled when considering larger initial errors. These findings not only validate Lorenz's qualitative intuition but also provide new quantitative insights into the predictability of turbulent flows, paving the way for potential applications in the prediction of extreme events.

</details>


### [9] [On the relationships between pressure geometrization and vortex identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11980)
*Han Tu,Hang Zhao,Qi Gao*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文将压力类比为引力，通过构建牛顿-嘉当几何并对空间度规作共形变换，把压力场重解释为诱导有效空间曲率（Ricci张量Rij与类Einstein张量Gij）的几何势，从而对流动进行几何化重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流体力学中的压力与广义相对论中的引力之间存在深刻的数学与概念类比，这一类比启发了对流动的几何化重新解释。作者希望借此建立一个统一框架，将压力理解为几何量而非外力，并统一解释涡识别准则与二维湍流双级串现象。

Method: 建立牛顿-嘉当几何，对空间度规施加共形变换；将压力场重解释为诱导有效空间曲率的几何势，编码于Ricci张量Rij及类Einstein张量Gij中；在弯曲有效空间中推导流体质点（仅受压力作用）沿测地线运动，并在弱场极限恢复经典Euler方程；由测地线方程与Bianchi恒等式导出压力Poisson方程；在退化二维极限下分析有效空间张量与总曲率积分。

Result: 仅受压力作用的流体质点沿测地线运动，弱场极限恢复Euler方程；压力Poisson方程由测地线方程和Bianchi恒等式自然涌现，表明压力是瞬时几何约束而非外力；主流Eulerian与Lagrangian涡识别准则对应于有效曲率的不同几何不变量或投影；二维退化极限下有效空间张量恒为零，总曲率全局积分亦为零，并据此给出二维湍流双级串的新解释：逆能量级串源于正曲率结构向大尺度的几何凝聚，正向拟涡能级串源于负曲率结构向小尺度的几何弥散。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了流动的统一几何解释，将压力几何化并揭示其为瞬时几何约束；统一了常用涡识别准则；并通过二维退化极限下的拓扑约束，为二维湍流双级串提供了基于曲率凝聚与弥散的新解释。

Abstract: The profound mathematical and conceptual analogy between pressure in fluid mechanics and gravity in general relativity motivates a novel geometric reinterpretation of flows. This article proposes a theoretical framework for geometrizing pressure by establishing a Newton-Cartan geometry and applying a conformal transformation to the spatial metric. The pressure field is reinterpreted as a geometric potential that induces an effective spatial curvature, encoded in the Ricci tensor Rij and the associated Einstein-like tensor Gij. Within this curved effective space, fluid particles under the exclusive influence of pressure follow geodesics, and the classical Euler equation is recovered in the weak-field limit. The pressure Poisson equation emerges naturally as a consequence of the geodesic equation and the Bianchi identity, revealing pressure as an instantaneous geometric constraint rather than an external force. This framework provides a unified geometric interpretation of widely used vortex identification criteria, demonstrating that the prevailing Eulerian and Lagrangian schemes correspond to different geometric invariants or projections of the effective curvature. In the degenerate two-dimensional (2D) limit, the effective spatial tensor vanishes identically, and the global integral of the total curvature vanishes as well. This topological constraint offers a novel interpretation of the dual-cascade scenario in 2D turbulence: the inverse energy cascade arises from geometric condensation of positive-curvature structures toward larger scales, while the forward enstrophy cascade results from geometric dispersion of negative-curvature structures toward smaller scales.

</details>


### [10] [Extending convolutional neural networks to finite-volume meshes for fine-scale reconstruction of wall-bounded turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12141)
*Hesam Tofighian,Jordan A. Denev,Nikolai Kornev*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 提出有限体积卷积单元（FVCU），使CNN超分辨率可直接作用于非均匀、非正交网格，并在湍流通道流与圆管流中实现精确的三维速度场重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准CNN湍流超分辨率方法受限于均匀结构网格，难以直接应用于壁面约束流动与复杂几何中常见的非均匀、非正交网格，因而亟需一种能在一般有限体积网格上定义的卷积算子。

Method: 将三维卷积精确表示为逐轴卷积的线性组合，并采用逐轴梯度、平均与恒等算子，这些算子可通过有限体积离散在任意多面体单元上求值，从而构造出FVCU，作为常规卷积层的即插即用替代。将FVCU嵌入超分辨率网络，从4倍粗化的低分辨率场重构湍流通道流（非均匀网格）与圆管流（非正交O型网格）的3D速度场；并借鉴LES近壁亚格子建模中Van Driest阻尼函数的作用，将其作为条件输入编码壁面距离，并通过消融实验验证其物理一致性。

Result: 在两种构型下，模型均能准确重构瞬时速度场、雷诺应力及其各向异性，以及超出低分辨率网格Nyquist极限的能谱；在训练中未见的椭圆管上，模型再现了主要流动特征与局部统计量，仅对小尺度能量有轻微高估。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了将基于CNN的超分辨率方法推广至一般有限体积网格的可行性，为复杂几何与壁面约束湍流的高保真重构提供了通用框架。

Abstract: Standard convolutional neural networks for turbulence super-resolution are restricted to uniform structured grids, preventing their direct application to the non-uniform and non-orthogonal meshes commonly used in wall-bounded flows and complex geometries. We introduce a finite-volume convolution unit (FVCU) that removes this restriction. The key idea is to construct convolutional layers using finite-volume operators that are applicable to non-uniform and unstructured meshes. To this end, we use an exact representation of a 3D convolution as a linear combination of successive axis-wise convolutions. We adopt axis-wise gradient, averaging, and identity operators, as they can be evaluated on arbitrary polyhedral cells using finite-volume discretization. The resulting FVCU is well-defined on general meshes, serving as a drop-in replacement for conventional convolution layers. The FVCU is embedded in a super-resolution neural network and trained to reconstruct 3D velocity fields from $4\times$ coarsened low-resolution fields in turbulent channel flow on a non-uniform mesh, and in circular-pipe flow on a non-orthogonal O-grid mesh. Inspired by the role of the Van Driest damping function in near-wall subgrid-scale modelling in large-eddy simulations, this function is supplied as a conditional input to encode wall proximity. An ablation study confirms that the network interprets this input in a physically consistent manner. In both configurations, the model accurately reconstructs instantaneous velocity fields, Reynolds-stresses and their anisotropy, and energy spectra beyond the Nyquist limit of the low-resolution mesh. On an elliptic pipe unseen during training, the model reproduces the main flow features and local statistics, with a modest overprediction of small-scale energy. These results demonstrate a framework for extending CNN-based super-resolution to general finite-volume grids.

</details>


### [11] [Online Learning of a Spectral Eddy Viscosity Closure for Large Eddy Simulation Using Ensemble Kalman Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12170)
*Katerina Kostova,Yifei Guan*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文利用集合卡尔曼反演（EKI）在线校准二维强迫湍流的谱粘性闭合模型，通过最小化LES与DNS动能谱失配，验证了EKI作为无导数、多参数数据驱动湍流闭合构建框架的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LES仅解析大尺度含能结构，未解析的亚格子尺度需通过闭合模型建模，因此LES精度高度依赖亚格子闭合的准确性；同时谱粘性闭合含多个参数，传统标定困难，需探索高效的数据驱动校准方法。

Method: 针对二维强迫湍流，采用谱粘性闭合，其形式为波数相关的粘性函数，并用16维向量参数化。将参数向量集合输入粗分辨率LES模拟，计算所得动能谱并与DNS参考谱在LES可解析波数区间内比较；利用EKI的集合统计迭代更新谱粘性参数，以减小模型与数据间的谱失配。

Result: 通过谱失配的收敛性评估校准后闭合的精度，在谱空间中将LES结果与滤波DNS数据进行对比，表明EKI能够有效迭代降低模型-数据差异。

Conclusion: EKI-based在线校准是一种无导数框架，可用于构建含多参数的数据驱动湍流闭合模型，展示了其在亚格子建模中的潜力。

Abstract: Large Eddy Simulation (LES) resolves only the large, energy-containing structures of turbulent flows while modeling the effects of unresolved subgrid scales. As a result, the ac- curacy of LES strongly depends on the subgrid-scale closure. This work investigates the online calibration of a spectral viscosity closure for forced two-dimensional turbulence using ensemble Kalman Inversion (EKI). The closure consists of a wavenumber-dependent viscosity function, which is parameterized by a 16-dimensional vector. An ensemble of potential parameter vec- tors is passed through coarse resolution LES simulations, and the resulting kinetic energy spec- tra are compared with a DNS reference spectrum up to the LES-resolved wavenumber range. Then, ensemble statistics iteratively update the spectral viscosity parameters in order to reduce the model-data discrepancy. The convergence of the spectral mismatch is used to assess the accuracy of the calibrated closure by comparing LES with the filtered DNS data in spectral space. The results demonstrate the potential of EKI-based online calibration as a derivative- free framework for constructing data-driven turbulence closures with multiple parameters.

</details>


### [12] [Learning minimum-time navigation policies in two-dimensional flows with a genetic algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12177)
*Vladimir Parfenyev*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文研究已知二维流场中固定滑移速度、可控方向船舶的最小时间导航问题，将导航策略参数化为神经网络，用无交叉的遗传算法优化，在多个基准（含二维湍流）上复现最优控制解并优于Q学习和单步actor-critic方法，且对起点变化和未解析小尺度湍流具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂流场中最小时间轨迹通常依赖解析或数值最优控制方法，计算成本高、难以处理高维或湍流情形；同时强化学习方法常需奖励塑形。因此作者希望寻找一种紧凑、易并行、无需奖励塑形的策略表示与优化方法。

Method: 将导航策略参数化为神经网络；采用遗传算法优化策略集合，通过达尔文选择与参数变异进化，不使用交叉；在若干基准案例及二维湍流中与解析解、数值最优控制解对比，并与Q-learning和单步actor-critic方法比较；进一步测试策略对起点变化和未解析小尺度湍流扰动的鲁棒性。

Result: 该方法在多个基准案例（包括二维湍流）中恢复了分析与数值最优控制解，并优于Q-learning和单步actor-critic方法；学习到的导航策略对起点变化和未解析小尺度湍流波动具有鲁棒性，前提是这些波动的特征速度相对船舶滑移速度较小。

Conclusion: 所提方法为复杂流场中的最小时间轨迹计算提供了一种紧凑表示、易并行且无需奖励塑形的高效替代方案。

Abstract: We study minimum-time navigation between two points in a known two-dimensional fluid flow for a vessel with fixed-magnitude slip velocity and controllable direction. The navigation policy is parameterized by a neural network and optimized using a genetic algorithm that evolves an ensemble of candidate strategies through Darwinian selection and parameter mutation, without crossover. The method recovers analytical and numerical optimal-control solutions in several benchmark cases, including two-dimensional turbulence, and outperforms Q-learning and one-step actor-critic method. We further show that the learned navigation strategies are robust to variations in the starting position and to unresolved small-scale turbulent fluctuations, provided that the characteristic velocity of these fluctuations remains small compared to the vessel's slip velocity. The proposed approach provides a compact representation of the navigation policy, is readily parallelizable, and eliminates the need for reward shaping, offering an efficient alternative to existing analytical and numerical methods for computing minimum-time trajectories in complex flows.

</details>

### [13] [SAVU-BENCH: A Real-World Benchmark for Spatial Audio-Visual Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10624)
*Yu Chen,Ruihang Liu,Yangguang Xu,Xinyue Jiang,Mohammed Bennamoun,Farid Boussaid,Xinyuan Qian,Qiuhong Ke*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出真实场景空间音视频理解基准SAVU-Bench、诊断集SAVU-Diag及免训练证据增强基线SAVU-EA，揭示音频空间感知是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多依赖仿真场景、仅评估孤立空间技能，且缺乏对失败模式的诊断能力，难以系统衡量真实场景下的空间音视频理解。

Method: 构建SAVU-Bench，覆盖三个能力层级、七项评估任务；设计场景关联诊断集SAVU-Diag，将推理问题分解为前提性定位与对齐子任务；基于诊断发现提出免训练证据增强基线SAVU-EA，使空间线索更显式。

Result: 在SAVU-Bench上评测12个代表性模型：视觉空间定位相对成熟，涉及音频的空间感知为主要瓶颈；SAVU-Diag显示多数推理错误与前提子任务失败共现，但前提正确时仍存在推理差距；SAVU-EA显著提升空间定位与联合匹配，但高层空间推理仍困难。

Conclusion: 亟需发展鲁棒的空间音频感知与更深层的跨模态空间关系整合能力。

Abstract: Spatial audio-visual understanding requires models to recognize not only what is present, but also where events occur and how they relate across modalities. Existing benchmarks often rely on simulated scenes, evaluate isolated spatial skills, and provide limited diagnostic insight into failure modes. We introduce SAVU-Bench, a real-world benchmark that systematically evaluates spatial audio-visual understanding across three capability levels and seven evaluation tasks. We further introduce SAVU-Diag, a scene-linked diagnostic set that decomposes reasoning questions into their prerequisite grounding and alignment sub-tasks. Evaluation of 12 representative models on SAVU-Bench reveals that while visual spatial grounding is relatively mature, spatial perception involving audio remains a primary bottleneck. SAVU-Diag further demonstrates that most reasoning errors co-occur with failures on these prerequisite tasks, though reasoning gaps persist even when prerequisites are correctly resolved. Motivated by these findings, we introduce SAVU-EA, a training-free evidence-augmented baseline that makes spatial cues more explicit. While SAVU-EA substantially improves spatial grounding and joint matching, high-level spatial reasoning remains challenging. Our findings highlight the urgent need for both robust spatial audio perception and deeper integration of cross-modal spatial relations.

</details>


### [14] [BEANS-Next and ROOTS: Broadening Audio-Language Capabilities for Bioacoustics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10663)
*Christos Plachouras,David Robinson,Marius Miron,Gagan Narula,Paul Laisné,Anthony L. T. Fine,Benno Weck,Ellen Gilsenan-McMahon,Diane Kim,Laura Hay Mack,Maddie Cusimano,Sara Keen,Lukas Rauch,Benjamin Hoffman,Emmanuel Chemla,Emmanouil Benetos,Johan Pauwels,Milad Alizadeh,Matthieu Geist,Olivier Pietquin*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 论文提出BEANS-Next基准（覆盖声学感知、生物类别识别、场景理解、上下文学习四类任务）揭示现有大型音频-语言模型在窄任务之外表现有限，并发布大规模训练资源ROOTS，显著提升模型在全部任务组的性能，开源基准、数据集与数据流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物声学与动物行为学涉及广泛的音频理解任务，但领域进展仅以狭窄任务（如物种与叫声类型分类等标签中心的生物类别识别）衡量，无法反映模型作为通用助手的能力，制约了其在更广生物声学应用中的价值。

Method: 构建基于生物声学任务分类学的BEANS-Next基准，涵盖声学感知、生物类别识别、场景理解与上下文学习四类任务族；并构建大规模训练资源ROOTS，整合扩充的策展真实数据、此前未被充分利用的行为与声学元数据，辅以音频派生信息，并在标注不足处采用可扩展的合成生成。

Result: 在BEANS-Next上评测显示，现有模型在既有评测强调的任务族之外性能受限；在ROOTS上训练后在BEANS-Next全部任务组上均取得实质性提升，使音频-语言模型更接近生物声学与动物行为学通用助手的目标。

Conclusion: 拓展任务空间与训练资源可推动生物声学通用音频理解发展；作者开源基准、数据集与数据流程以加速该领域研究。

Abstract: Bioacoustics and ethology encompass a wide range of audio understanding tasks, many of which stand to benefit from recent advances in large audio-language models. However, progress in the field has so far been assessed on a narrow set of tasks, primarily centered on label-centric biological category recognition, such as species and call-type classification. In this work, we introduce BEANS-Next, a benchmark grounded in a taxonomy of bioacoustics tasks spanning acoustic perception, biological category recognition, scene understanding, and in-context learning. Using BEANS-Next, we show that existing models exhibit limited performance beyond the task families emphasized by existing evaluations, constraining their usefulness for broader bioacoustic applications. To support progress on this broader task space, we also introduce ROOTS, a large-scale training resource built from expanded curated real-world data and previously underused behavioral and acoustic metadata, supplemented by audio-derived information and scalable synthetic generation where labeling is insufficient. We demonstrate that training on this dataset yields substantial progress across all task groups of BEANS-Next, moving audio-language models closer to their potential as general-purpose assistants for bioacoustics and ethology. To accelerate progress in the field, we open-source our benchmark, dataset, and data pipelines.

</details>


### [15] [Listen-to-Reason: Listen with Experts, Retrieve over a Graph, Reason with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10749)
*Pooneh Mousavi,Mirco Ravanelli,Cem Subakan*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出 Listen-to-Reason（L2R）可解释音频理解流水线：冻结专家编码器上的小型头将音频片段映射到显式语义树节点，冻结纯文本 LLM 依据节点与 ASR 转写作答，实现答案可溯源与因果可验证，并以极低训练成本取得有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LALM 将音频编码器与 LLM 多阶段耦合训练，导致更换领域或更强 LLM 都需重训练，且答案无法追溯到实际听到的音频内容，思维链仅是事后解释，缺乏可解释性与可验证性。

Method: L2R 采用 by-design 可解释流水线：在冻结的专家编码器（语音、音乐、环境声）上训练小型头，将音频片段各 chunk 映射到人类可读语义树的节点；冻结的纯文本 LLM 仅依据这些节点和 ASR 转写回答问题，不直接听音频。任一 LLM 均可作为 reader，新领域仅需增加一个小头并用少量标注片段训练。

Result: 在 SAKURA 上，7B reader 的 L2R 优于所有对比 LALM，且节点具因果性——替换决定性节点可使 78% 正确答案翻转；在 MMAU 与 MMAR 上落后 6–12 分，但训练参数量少约 1400 倍、音频数据量少数个数量级。更强 reader 无需重训音频组件即可缩小差距；新领域每物种仅 5 个标注片段时，比同片段 QLoRA 微调 LALM 高 13–26 分。

Conclusion: L2R 以解耦、可解释、可溯源的树式中间表示替代端到端音频-语言耦合，实现因果可验证的推理与高效跨领域、跨 reader 扩展，证明可解释设计与强性能可兼得。

Abstract: Large audio-language models (LALMs) fuse an audio encoder into a large language model (LLM) through multi-stage training. This coupling means that a new domain or a stronger LLM requires retraining, and their answers cannot be traced to what the model heard: a chain-of-thought is a post-hoc account. We propose Listen-to-Reason (L2R), an interpretable-by-design pipeline that passes audio to the LLM through an explicit, human-readable tree: small heads on frozen expert encoders map each chunk of a clip to semantically meaningful nodes on the tree (for speech, music and environmental sound), and a frozen text-only LLM answers from these nodes and an ASR transcript without hearing the clip. Every answer can therefore be traced to the nodes and transcript it read, and the nodes are causal: replacing the deciding node with a distractor overturns 78% of correct answers on SAKURA. With a 7B reader, L2R outperforms all LALMs we compare against on SAKURA and trails them by 6-12 points on MMAU and MMAR, despite training about 1,400x fewer parameters on orders of magnitude less audio data. However, because any LLM can serve as the reader, we show that a stronger reader narrows this gap without retraining any audio component. A new domain is added with one small head: with five labelled clips per species, it outperforms QLoRA fine-tuning of an LALM on the same clips by 13-26 points.

</details>


### [16] [AutoSynth: Learning to Generate Editable Synthesizer Programs from Audio and Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10774)
*Tristan Wu,Daniel Chin,Liwei Lin,Junan Zhang,Gus Xia*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: AutoSynth将MIDI演奏事件、固定合成器参数与变长调制路径统一为合成器序列，用音频条件自回归模型学习依赖，单一模型同时支持参考音频→合成器程序（合成器反演）与文本→程序（借助预训练音频生成模型）两类任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频生成模型输出波形，受音频压缩限制质量，且难以直接编辑音符、音色参数或调制关系；同时缺乏成对的文本-目标程序标注与可微合成器。

Method: 统一序列表示MIDI事件、固定合成器参数与变长调制路径；音频条件自回归建模。两阶段训练：先用少量原生预设自动构造大规模音频-程序对做监督学习，再用组相对策略优化（GRPO），混合奖励包含语义相似度、音高相关特征、声学相似度与声音可用性。

Result: 能够生成完整、可编辑的合成器程序；在合成器反演与文本驱动生成两个任务上均取得有竞争力的结果。

Conclusion: AutoSynth无需成对文本-目标程序标注，也无需可微合成器，即可实现可编辑的合成器程序生成，并可同时覆盖合成器反演与文本驱动音频生成。

Abstract: Audio generation models can translate natural-language descriptions into sound, but their outputs are typically waveforms. Their audio quality is constrained by audio compression, and their outputs do not readily support direct edits to notes, timbral parameters, or modulation relationships. We present AutoSynth, which represents MIDI performance events, fixed synthesizer parameters, and variable-length modulation routes as a unified sequence for a synthesizer, and learns their dependencies with an audio-conditioned autoregressive model. A single model supports both tasks. Given reference audio, the model directly predicts a synthesizer program; given text, it uses a pretrained audio generation model and converts the generated audio into a program. Training consists of two stages: supervised learning on large-scale audio-program pairs automatically constructed from a small set of native presets, followed by group-relative policy optimization with a mixed reward combining semantic similarity, pitch-related features, acoustic similarity, and sound usefulness. The pipeline requires neither paired text-target-program annotations nor a differentiable synthesizer. Experiments show that AutoSynth produces complete, editable synthesizer programs and achieves competitive results in both synthesizer inversion and text-driven generation. Audio demos and source code are available at https://auto-synth.github.io/.

</details>


### [17] [Cross-Lingual Speaker Verification with Self-Supervised Pre-Trained Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11099)
*Jinghan Peng,Yu Zheng,Weiqiang Wang,Jian Liu*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 利用大规模自监督预训练模型作为前端特征提取器，学习语言无关的说话人表征，以缓解跨语言说话人验证性能下降问题，在TidyVoice2026基准上取得EER 2.21%（eval-A）与2.99%（eval-U）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨语言场景下，说话人身份信息与语言相关的声学线索相互纠缠，导致说话人验证（SV）性能显著退化。作者希望解耦说话人身份与语言特异性特征，从而提升语言失配条件下的SV鲁棒性。

Method: 采用大规模自监督预训练模型（PTM）作为鲁棒的前端特征提取器，借助其从大规模多样化音频中习得的丰富声学与语言学知识获得泛化特征；随后将这些特征用于训练下游说话人嵌入网络，以有效解耦说话人身份与语言特异性表征。

Result: 在TidyVoice2026跨语言说话人验证基准上进行验证，所提系统（团队T02）在tv26_eval-A上EER为2.21%，在tv26_eval-U上EER为2.99%。

Conclusion: 以自监督预训练模型为前端、结合下游说话人嵌入训练的方案，可有效缓解语言失配带来的性能退化，验证了预训练表征在跨语言说话人验证中的有效性。

Abstract: Speaker verification (SV) performance degrades under language mismatch due to the entanglement of speaker identity with language-specific acoustic cues. To address this problem, we leverage large-scale self-supervised pre-trained models (PTMs) to learn language-agnostic speaker representations. We utilize PTMs as robust front-end feature extractors, capitalizing on their rich acoustic and linguistic knowledge acquired from vast, diverse audio data. These generalized features are then used to train a downstream speaker embedding network, effectively disentangling speaker identity from language-specific characteristics. We validate our approach on the TidyVoice2026 benchmark, which benchmarks SV under language mismatch. Our proposed system (team T02) achieves equal error rates (EERs) of 2.21% on tv26_eval-A and 2.99% on tv26_eval-U.

</details>


### [18] [MiDashengLM-Spatial: Unifying General Audio Understanding and Spatial Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11156)
*Jinbo Hu,Hang Su,Lichun Fan,Heinrich Dinkel,Gang Li,Zhanchen Dai,Yiru Zhang,Chang Liu,Peng Wang,Junnan Wu,Jian Luan,Cong Zou,Heng Qu*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出首个开源端到端统一音频语言模型MiDashengLM-Spatial，在单一架构内同时支持通用音频理解与空间感知，兼顾单声道通用性能与空间理解/推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型音频语言模型多为单声道设计，丢弃了空间感知所需的通道间线索；而现有空间音频语言模型专为空间任务定制，无法利用单声道LALM的通用理解能力。二者割裂，缺乏统一的端到端方案。

Method: 在MiDashengLM基础上引入空间音频编码器Spatial-Dasheng，通过分层语义到空间条件化模块，在多个深度将中间语义表示注入空间分支，同时保留原始语义通路；并构建数据合成流水线，渲染多样化空间声学场景，生成场景级空间描述与问答对以提供大规模空间音频-语言监督。

Result: Spatial-Dasheng在真实场景的声事件定位与检测上表现强劲；MiDashengLM-Spatial在空间理解与推理基准上显著优于现有LALMs；在多样化单声道基准上与最先进的8B规模LALMs保持竞争力，表明在不损害通用音频理解的前提下可获取空间感知能力。

Conclusion: 空间感知与通用音频理解可在单一统一架构中协同获得，为开源统一空间音频语言模型提供了可行路径；源码与模型检查点已公开。

Abstract: Large audio-language models (LALMs) have achieved strong performance in general audio understanding, yet most are designed for monaural input and discard the inter-channel cues essential for spatial perception. In contrast, existing spatial audio-language models are purpose-built for spatial tasks and fail to capitalize on the general understanding capabilities of monaural LALMs. We present MiDashengLM-Spatial, the first open-source end-to-end unified audio-language model, to our knowledge, which supports both general audio understanding and spatial awareness within a single architecture. It extends MiDashengLM with a spatial audio encoder, Spatial-Dasheng, integrated through a hierarchical semantic-to-spatial conditioning module that injects intermediate semantic representations into the spatial branch at multiple depths while preserving the original semantic pathway. To provide spatial audio-language supervision at scale, we develop a data synthesis pipeline that renders diverse spatial acoustic scenes with scene-level spatial descriptions and question-answer pairs. Experiments show that Spatial-Dasheng achieves strong performance on sound event localization and detection in real-world scenes, and that MiDashengLM-Spatial substantially outperforms existing LALMs on spatial understanding and reasoning benchmarks. Meanwhile, it remains competitive with state-of-the-art 8B-scale LALMs on diverse monaural benchmarks, demonstrating that spatial awareness can be acquired without compromising general audio understanding. The source code and model checkpoint are available at https://github.com/xiaomi-research/midashenglm-spatial and https://huggingface.co/mispeech/midashenglm-spatial.

</details>


### [19] [Selective Listening: Mechanism-Guided Control of Audio Influence in Large Audio-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11196)
*Yulin Sun,Kele Xu,Yong Dou*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 论文提出ICAP-Gate：通过机制引导、任务条件化的后期音频通路控制，抑制无关音频对推理的干扰，同时保留显式音频需求任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型音频语言模型（LALMs）会利用多模态证据，但当任务本身不需要听觉输入时，无关音频仍会改变文本推理决策。聚合准确率会掩盖这种"配对漂移"，因为音频造成的修复与损害可能相互抵消，因此需要成对分析与针对性干预来定位可控制点。

Method: 采用配对漂移分析与定向干预，识别出架构特异、对干预敏感的后期音频通路作为控制点；据此提出ICAP-Gate，对各模型通路施加机制引导、任务条件化的控制（仅在有显式音频需求时保留该通路）。

Result: 在4个LALMs、2个推理基准、环境声与自然语音干扰下，ICAP-Gate在全部16个全量划分的模型—条件评估中，Influence Rate与Answer Flip点估计均低于无门控推理；固定抑制在四个模型上均损害ASR，而ICAP-Gate通过为显式音频指令保留通路，达到与无门控相当的ASR性能；其配对漂移点估计在四个设置中均低于缓解提示；相比八样本Self-Consistency，以每查询一次生成达到有竞争力的稳定性，而Self-Consistency在ARC上带来7.0–9.2倍于无门控的延迟。

Conclusion: 结果确立了"选择性模态影响控制"作为稳健多模态推理的设计原则。

Abstract: Large audio-language models (LALMs) exploit multimodal evidence, yet task-irrelevant audio can alter text-reasoning decisions when listening is unnecessary. Aggregate Accuracy can hide this paired drift because audio-induced repairs and damages may cancel. Paired drift analysis and targeted interventions identify architecture-specific, intervention-sensitive late audio pathways as actionable control points. We introduce ICAP-Gate, which applies mechanism-guided, task-conditioned control to each model's pathway. Across four LALMs, two reasoning benchmarks, and environmental-sound and natural-speech interference, ICAP-Gate has lower point estimates for Influence Rate and Answer Flip than ungated inference in all 16 full-split model--condition evaluations. Fixed suppression degrades automatic speech recognition (ASR) across all four models, whereas ICAP-Gate matches ungated ASR performance by preserving the pathway for explicit audio-demand instructions. ICAP-Gate has lower paired-drift point estimates than mitigation prompting in all four evaluated settings and provides competitive stabilization relative to eight-sample Self-Consistency while using one generation per query; in controlled ARC measurements, Self-Consistency incurs $7.0$--$9.2\times$ ungated latency. These results establish selective modality influence control as a design principle for robust multimodal reasoning.

</details>


### [20] [A computational framework for the acoustic characterization of Suikinkutsu (water harp cave)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11219)
*Paul Haimes,Alarith Uhde,Daniel Moritz Marutschke*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本研究提出一个计算框架，利用FFT、MFCC、RMS能量、频谱质心、频谱带宽与频谱对比度等特征，对日本关西地区9处水琴窟的现场录音进行频谱与时域分析，实现系统化声学比较，为水琴窟的数字化保存提供方法基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水琴窟是日本寺院庭园中毗邻蹲踞石水钵的半隐藏式声学装置，既有研究多聚焦其物理声学与文化意义，而面向其声学特征的计算化记录与比较方法仍显不足。

Method: 采集关西地区寺院、神社与庭园中9处水琴窟的现场录音，构建特征提取流程，纳入快速傅里叶变换（FFT）、梅尔频率倒谱系数（MFCC）、均方根（RMS）能量、频谱质心、频谱带宽与频谱对比度，并以多维计算描述符刻画其声学轮廓；同时进行场地内与场地间变异性分析。该方法不试图反演单个水琴窟的物理几何结构。

Result: 提取特征显示，各水琴窟虽在频谱与音色上大体相似，但均呈现可测量的独特声学轮廓；场地内与场地间变异性分析表明，这些轮廓反映的是有界范围内的特征性共振行为，而非固定的声学签名。

Conclusion: 该框架是首批系统化表征与比较水琴窟的计算方法之一，为后续研究及这一日本环境设计形式的数字化保存提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Suikinkutsu are partially concealed acoustic devices traditionally installed adjacent to stone water basins (tsukubai) in Japanese temple gardens. While previous research has examined their physical acoustics and cultural significance, computational methods for documenting and comparing their acoustic characteristics remain underexplored. This exploratory study introduces a computational framework for analyzing suikinkutsu using spectral and temporal audio features extracted from field recordings. Rather than estimating the physical geometry of individual suikinkutsu, the framework characterizes their acoustic profiles using a multidimensional set of computational descriptors, enabling systematic comparisons between recordings across different sites and installations. Audio recordings from nine suikinkutsu located at temples, shrines, and gardens in the Kansai region of Japan were analyzed using a feature-extraction pipeline that incorporates Fast Fourier Transform (FFT), Mel-Frequency Cepstral Coefficients (MFCCs), Root Mean Square (RMS) energy, spectral centroid, spectral bandwidth, and spectral contrast. The extracted features indicate that, although the suikinkutsu share broadly similar spectral and timbral characteristics, each exhibits a measurably distinct acoustic profile; a supplementary analysis of within-site and between-site variability suggests these profiles reflect a bounded range of characteristic resonant behaviour rather than a fixed acoustic signature. The proposed framework provides one of the first computational approaches for systematically characterizing and comparing suikinkutsu, contributing a practical foundation for future research and the digital preservation of this distinctive form of Japanese environmental design.

</details>


### [21] [Edit Who Speaks, Control How They Speak: Global Timbre Editing and Local Instruction Control for TTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11437)
*Junchuan Zhao,Chenglin Xu,Wei Zeng,Haoyang Li,Yiwen Guo,Ye Wang*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: EDICT 提出一个统一框架，通过编辑后的声学参考在分段合成中锚定音色身份，实现全局音色编辑与局部表达控制的结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令式 TTS 中，声音克隆只能复现参考音色而无法修改，文本声音设计只能从描述生成新音色；两者都无法直接对给定参考音色进行修改并合成。此外，话语级表达指令无法明确各文本片段的具体变化。

Method: 将参考音频与结构化音色编辑结合，在 codec-token 空间生成编辑参考，作为冻结 TTS 骨干的共享声音锚点；片段级自然语言指令引导表达；在每个片段边界重建 KV cache，刷新指令条件同时保留有界声学上下文。

Result: 在提出的 TimbreEdit-Bench 与 IntraTTS-Bench 上评估，显示音色编辑改善，并在局部指令遵循、说话人一致性与过渡质量之间取得良好平衡。

Conclusion: EDICT 统一了全局音色编辑与局部表达控制，为指令式 TTS 提供兼顾音色修改、表达控制和声学连续性的可行方案。

Abstract: Instruction-based text-to-speech (TTS) offers control over voice characteristics and speech expression through interfaces including voice cloning and text-based voice design. Voice cloning reproduces a reference voice, whereas text-based voice design creates a voice from a natural-language description. However, neither interface directly enables users to modify the timbre of a given reference and synthesize speech with the modified voice. Meanwhile, utterance-level expressive instructions leave changes across individual text segments underspecified. We introduce \textbf{EDICT}, a framework that unifies global timbre editing and local expressive control by using an edited acoustic reference to anchor voice identity across segments. To enable synthesis with an instruction-edited voice, EDICT combines reference audio with structured timbre edits to generate an edited reference in codec-token space. This representation serves as a shared voice anchor for a frozen TTS backbone, allowing segment-specific natural-language instructions to guide expression. To accommodate instruction changes while supporting acoustic continuity, EDICT rebuilds the KV cache at each segment boundary, refreshing instruction conditioning while retaining bounded acoustic context from previously generated speech. Evaluations on our proposed TimbreEdit-Bench and IntraTTS-Bench demonstrate improved timbre editing and a favorable balance between local instruction adherence, speaker consistency, and transition quality. Audio demos are available.

</details>


### [22] [SepGen: Multi-Stem Audio-Video Separation and Generation in a Single Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11361)
*Aviad Dahan,Rajaei Khatib,Yonatan Bitton,Idan Szpektor,Lior Wolf,Raja Giryes*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: SepGen 扩展预训练音视频生成器，在一次联合采样中同时生成视频、混合音轨以及每个有字幕描述声源的独立波形，支持生成与分离两种模式，并在语音分离等任务上超越语言条件分离器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D 音视频场景由视频、动态几何和具有各自位置与轨迹的声源组成。从新视角渲染这类场景要求每个声源都是独立波形，以便先定位、传播再混合。然而现有联合音视频生成器只输出单一混合音轨，声源不可单独获取，无法满足这一需求。

Method: 提出 SepGen，在预训练音视频生成器基础上扩展，使其在一次联合采样中输出视频、混合音轨以及每个带字幕描述声源的单条波形。模型支持两种互补模式：生成模式下每条声源字幕规定对应 stem 的内容（如双人对话按原始轮次输出每位说话人一个 stem）；分离模式下输入混合音频保持干净，由字幕指定要提取的内容，从而可依据自由文本描述分解录音。

Result: 生成模式在文本合成场景上评估，分离模式在其它生成器渲染的场景及真实语音、音乐和音效录音上评估。给定混合音频与含台词的字幕时，SepGen 优于语言条件分离器，在语音上优势最明显；即使从字幕中移除台词，仍保持领先。

Conclusion: SepGen 通过联合采样实现音视频生成与声源分离的统一，使声源以独立波形形式可访问，兼顾生成与分离两种应用，并在语音、音乐、音效等任务上取得优于基线分离器的性能。代码、检查点和数据集已开源。

Abstract: A 4D audio-visual scene comprises a video, the dynamic geometry it depicts, and the sound sources that populate it, each with its own position and trajectory. Rendering such a scene from a novel viewpoint requires that every source be available as an individual waveform, so that it can be localized in the scene and propagated to the observer before the signals are mixed. Joint audio-video generators can synthesize the video and its soundtrack, but the soundtrack is emitted as a single audio-mix in which the sources are not individually accessible. We present SepGen, which extends a pretrained audio-video generator to emit the video, the mixed soundtrack, and one waveform per captioned source in one joint sampling run. SepGen supports two complementary modes: generation and separation. In generation mode, each source caption specifies what its stem contains. A two-speaker dialogue, for example, comes out as one stem per speaker in the original turn order. In separation mode, the input audio-mix remains clean while the captions specify what to extract, so the model can decompose a recording from a free-text description. We evaluate generation on scenes synthesized from text, and separation on scenes rendered by other generators and on real recordings of speech, music, and sound effects. Given an audio-mix and captions that carry the spoken lines, SepGen outperforms language-conditioned separators, most clearly on speech, and it keeps the lead when the lines are removed from the captions. Code, checkpoints, and datasets are available at https://sepgen.github.io/

</details>


### [23] [Beyond Speech Captions: Speech-Rewarded Style Planning for Conversational Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11461)
*Shiao Zhu,Lianbo Liu,Sizhen Lyu,Yuzhe Wang,Sheng Li,Takahiro Shinozaki*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 论文提出Speech-Rewarded Style Planning (SRSP)，通过冻结的下游TTS模型以GRPO强化学习训练文本风格规划器，缓解自然语言描述作为伪标签时"描述保真≠合成可控"的失配问题，在CoT-TTS语料英文子集上提升了风格与情感相似度并降低梅尔倒谱失真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言风格描述为LLM与可控TTS之间提供了可解释接口，但将描述作为伪标签会把目标声学信息压缩进文本，且描述的保真度并不必然意味着对特定合成器的有效控制。作者实证发现：语音-文本对齐度对同一话语下候选指令的下游声学相似度仅有微弱预测能力，说明仅靠文本层面监督不足。

Method: 提出SRSP，以冻结的下游TTS模型为奖励来源训练文本风格规划器。规划器输入对话历史与回复文本，生成候选指令，采用组相对策略优化（GRPO）进行优化，奖励为教师强制下目标语音token的似然度。

Result: 在ISCSLP 2026 CoT-TTS语料英文子集上，SRSP相比Base LLM与目标音频信息驱动的描述生成基线，取得更高的语音风格与情感相似度，以及更低的梅尔倒谱失真；基于LLM的表达性语音评测显示其在上下文恰当性与参考一致性上均优于所有基线。

Conclusion: 将风格规划置于下游合成器的声学反馈闭环中优化，比单纯依赖文本-语音对齐的描述生成更有效地实现可控、表达性TTS。

Abstract: Natural-language style descriptions provide an interpretable interface between large language models (LLMs) and controllable text-to-speech (TTS). However, using descriptions as pseudo-labels compresses target acoustics into text, and descriptive fidelity need not imply effective control of a particular synthesizer. We empirically show that speech-text alignment only weakly predicts downstream acoustic similarity among candidate instructions for the same utterance. We therefore propose Speech-Rewarded Style Planning (SRSP), which trains a text-based style planner through a frozen downstream TTS model. Given dialogue history and response text, the planner generates candidate instructions and is optimized with group-relative policy optimization (GRPO), using the teacher-forced likelihood of target speech tokens as the reward. On an English subset of the ISCSLP 2026 CoT-TTS corpus, SRSP achieves higher speech-style and emotion similarity to target speech and lower mel-cepstral distortion than the Base LLM and target-audio-informed captioning baselines. LLM-based expressive speech evaluation further shows gains over all baselines in contextual appropriateness and reference consistency.

</details>


### [24] [Reference-Free Singing Pitch Correction via Music-Constrained Sequence Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11524)
*Biao Dong,Jiajun Li,Binzhen Zhu,Mingwei Yi,Tong Liu,Yuanhao Zhang,Jiqing Han,Yongjun He*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出无参考歌声音高修正方法，将其建模为音乐约束的序列编辑任务，仅凭输入演唱即可判断并修正每个音符音高，无需目标旋律或伴奏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有歌声音高修正方法依赖目标旋律或伴奏轨，而实际场景中这些信息往往不可得，因此需要一种无参考（reference-free）的修正方案。

Method: 采用预训练符号音乐编码器加轻量歌声域适配器，生成演唱MIDI的上下文表示；基于该表示，两个轻量修正头通过分解式音高编辑分布联合建模“是否需要修正”与“带符号的音高偏移量”；再用由估计调性分布导出的输入相关调性先验对候选偏移进行重排序，倾向选择调性兼容的修正，无需目标旋律或真实调性标注。

Conclusion: 该方法验证了无参考歌声音高修正的可行性，通过分解式编辑分布与调性先验重排序，在无目标旋律与真实调性标注条件下有效提升音高准确率，并兼顾纠错与保留。

Abstract: Existing singing pitch correction approaches rely on target melodies or accompaniment tracks, which may be unavailable in practice. We formulate reference-free singing pitch correction as a music-constrained sequence editing task that determines whether and how each note should be corrected from the input performance alone. A pretrained symbolic music encoder with lightweight singing-domain adapters produces contextual representations of the singing MIDI. Based on these representations, two lightweight correction heads jointly model correction necessity and signed pitch modification through a factorized pitch-editing distribution. An input-dependent tonal prior derived from the estimated key distribution then reranks the candidate offsets, favoring tonally compatible corrections without target melodies or ground-truth key annotations. Experiments on real paired amateur and professional singing recordings show that the method improves note-level pitch accuracy from 73.25 to 83.43, outperforming a context-based baseline by 3.99 percentage points while balancing error correction and preservation of correctly performed notes.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在两个LALM上使用STEMMA-Instruct微调后，多音频推理能力得到提升，其中在可由曲库直接确定的结构性关系上增益最大，同时保持了单音频音乐理解能力。

Conclusion: 以制作溯源为核心、关系优先构建的多音频音乐问答框架，可有效弥补现有单音频或无关曲目比较范式在关系推理上的不足，并在不损害单音频理解的前提下提升多音频推理性能。

Abstract: Music understanding often requires comparing excerpts and reasoning about relationships among songs, sections, and stems. However, existing large audio-language models (LALMs) and music question-answering datasets typically operate on single recordings or compare independently sampled tracks with no known production relationship. We introduce STEMMA, a multi-audio music question-answering framework built around production provenance: whether excerpts originate from the same track or section, and which stems belong to which mixtures. Because such relations are sparse under conventional audio-first sampling, STEMMA adopts a relation-first construction strategy: it first specifies a target relation and then queries the catalog for excerpts that satisfy it and hard negatives that do not. Labels are determined directly from catalog provenance rather than generated by a language model from metadata. We build STEMMA-Bench for evaluation and a track-disjoint training set, STEMMA-Instruct. Fine-tuning two LALMs on STEMMA-Instruct improves multi-audio reasoning, with the largest gains on structural relations directly determined by the catalog, while preserving single-audio music understanding.

</details>


### [26] [DiffuPlex: Accelerating Full-Duplex Spoken Dialog Models via Rolling Masked Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12214)
*Heeseung Kim*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: DiffuPlex 提出一种滚动掩码扩散框架，通过一次骨干唤醒预测多个未来帧，显著降低全双工语音对话模型的顺序计算量，同时基本保持交互行为与通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全双工语音对话模型虽然支持边听边说，但细粒度模型在每个交互帧仍需自回归推进骨干网络，顺序计算开销大，成为部署瓶颈。

Method: 采用滚动掩码扩散（rolling masked diffusion）框架，单次骨干唤醒预测多个未来用户与助手帧；仅消费每个预测未来中的高置信前缀，并以原帧率持续交互。当用户语音到达时校验对应用户预测，若交互发生偏离，则保留已播放的助手内容、仅修正未播放的未来。基于同一预测器设计两种推理策略：DiffuPlex-LISTEN 在预测助手静音时消费多个未来帧；DiffuPlex-SPEAK 还能消费预测的助手语音。

Result: 在全双工交互与口语语言评测中，DiffuPlex-LISTEN 与 DiffuPlex-SPEAK 分别实现 1.46× 和 1.59× 的部署路径墙钟加速，以及 1.61× 和 1.80× 的 Core LM 加速；所有测量的骨干调用均在 80ms 交互间隔内完成。人工评测显示 LISTEN 保持语音自然度与会话质量，SPEAK 在保持会话质量的同时语音自然度略有下降。

Conclusion: DiffuPlex 通过扩散式多帧预测与置信前缀消费，有效缓解全双工对话骨干的顺序计算瓶颈，在保持交互行为与通用能力的前提下实现显著加速，为实时全双工语音对话提供可行的效率优化路径。

Abstract: Recent full-duplex spoken dialog models enable simultaneous listening and speaking, but fine-grained models still advance their backbone autoregressively at every interaction frame. We introduce DiffuPlex, a rolling masked diffusion framework that reduces this sequential computation by predicting multiple future user and assistant frames in a single backbone wake. DiffuPlex consumes only a confident prefix of each predicted future while interaction continues at the original frame rate. As user speech arrives, it checks the corresponding user predictions and, when the interaction diverges, preserves already played assistant content while revising only the unplayed future. We consider two inference policies over the same predictor: DiffuPlex-LISTEN consumes multiple future frames when they predict assistant silence, whereas DiffuPlex-SPEAK can also consume predicted assistant speech. Across full-duplex interaction and spoken-language evaluations, DiffuPlex substantially reduces sequential backbone computation while largely preserving interaction behavior and general capability. DiffuPlex-LISTEN and DiffuPlex-SPEAK achieve $1.46\times$ and $1.59\times$ deployment-path wall-clock speedups and $1.61\times$ and $1.80\times$ Core LM speedups, with all measured backbone invocations completing within the 80ms interaction interval. Human evaluation shows that LISTEN preserves speech naturalness and conversational quality, while SPEAK retains conversational quality with some degradation in speech naturalness.

</details>

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文通过配对源重建研究预训练音频编码器保留的信号信息，用统一的Stable Audio Open潜在扩散解码器从冻结表征重建音乐，发现不同编码器族重建能力差异明显，且暴露更细的时频结构可提升恢复效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练音频编码器常被复用于训练时未知的下游任务，其有用性部分取决于哪些信号属性在预训练目标中得以保留。作者希望探究编码器表征中保留下来的源信息。

Method: 采用配对源重建方法，使用共享的Stable Audio Open潜在扩散解码器，从冻结表征重建5秒、44.1kHz立体声音乐。编码器涵盖监督分类器（VGGish、ConvNeXt）、音频-文本对比模型（CLAP）和波形重建模型（EnCodec）。这些目标对保留源细节施加不同压力，其暴露接口在时频分辨率上差异显著，评估在Million Song Dataset（MSD）上进行。

Result: 不同编码器族的可重建性存在明显差异；在编码器内部比较中，暴露更细的时间或频谱结构时恢复效果更好。即便压缩的面向任务嵌入也能支持保留可测量的源特异性和高层音乐内容的重建。

Conclusion: 预训练音频编码器保留的信息取决于预训练目标及接口的时频分辨率，向任务型嵌入仍保留可重建源特异性和高级音乐内容的能力，表明下游可用性与编码器暴露的信号结构密切相关。

Abstract: Pretrained audio encoders are reused for downstream tasks that are often unknown when the encoder is trained, so their usefulness depends partly on which signal properties survive the pretext objective. We study this retained information through paired source reconstruction. Using a shared Stable Audio Open latent diffusion decoder, we reconstruct five-second, 44.1-kHz stereo music from frozen representations produced by supervised classifiers (VGGish, ConvNeXt), an audio-text contrastive model (CLAP), and a waveform-reconstruction model (EnCodec). These objectives impose different pressures to preserve source detail, while their exposed interfaces vary substantially in temporal and spectral resolution. Evaluating on the Million Song Dataset (MSD), we find clear differences in reconstructability across encoder families, while within encoder comparisons show improved recovery when finer temporal or spectral structure is exposed. Even compressed task oriented embeddings support reconstructions that preserve measurable source specificity and high level musical content.

</details>

TL;DR: 提出"响应矩阵"框架，通过对系统本身施加受控干预与观测，揭示环境中隐藏记忆的时间结构，实现并行化数值求解，并在自旋过程与二量子比特基准上验证量子记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子系统难以与外界环境隔离，隐藏环境会保留并延迟返还信息，导致量子计算中出现相关错误，并使实验解释复杂化。环境通常无法直接测量，且完整层析重构其效应很快变得不可行，因此需要一种仅依赖系统观测即可刻画环境记忆的方法。

Method: 引入"响应矩阵"，通过受控干预与系统观测记录不同受控历史下未来观测的变化；利用矩阵谱刻画记忆结构及其在指定系统重置下的持续方式；通过对矩阵元素的适当线性组合排除特定的经典记忆模型；由于历史-未来查询可独立评估，数值响应矩阵可并行组装。

Result: 在理论上建立了该框架，在相互作用自旋过程中刻画了响应谱，并在二量子比特基准上认证了量子记忆的存在。

Conclusion: 该方法将系统观测转化为探测不可及环境的工具，有望支持相关噪声诊断、开放系统模型检验以及记忆感知的控制设计。

Abstract: Quantum systems are rarely isolated from their surroundings. These hidden environments can retain information and return it later, producing correlated errors in quantum computers and complicating the interpretation of experiments.On a quantum processor, for example, one job can therefore leave an environmental trace that affects the next, even after the qubits themselves have been reset. Learning how this hidden influence persists and affects later behavior is therefore important, but the environment is often impossible to measure directly, and fully reconstructing its effects through tomography rapidly becomes impractical. Here, we introduce a response matrix that uses controlled interventions and observations of the system alone to reveal the temporal structure of environmental memory. The matrix records how future observations change across different controlled histories. Its spectrum characterizes how memory is structured and how it persists under specified system resets, while suitable linear combinations of its entries can rule out specified classical models of that memory. Because its history-future queries can be evaluated independently, numerical response matrices can be assembled in parallel. We establish the framework theoretically, characterize response spectra in an interacting spin process, and certify quantum memory in a two-qubit benchmark. This approach turns system observations into a probe of inaccessible environments and could support the diagnosis of correlated noise, tests of open system models, and memory-aware control design.

</details>

*Francesco Perciavalle,Agostino Gallo,Francesco Plastina,Gianluigi Greco,Nicola Lo Gullo,Carlo Adornetto*

TL;DR: 本文将量子动力学建模为时间序列预测问题，以Rydberg原子阵列可实现的少比特PXP链为基准，比较Transformer与DLinear。结果显示简单线性模型DLinear在全部初态下均保持高精度，而Transformer在接近疤痕极限时性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典方法难以准确模拟混沌量子多体动力学，原因是演化过程中纠缠快速增长并在空间扩散。作者由此提出核心问题：机器学习能否为量子动力学预测提供有效替代方案，并需要在定性不同的动力学区间内系统评估模型表现。

Method: 将量子动力学表述为时间序列预测任务，基准系统为可用Rydberg原子阵列实现的少比特PXP链。通过改变初始态，使系统覆盖从遍历行为到量子多体疤痕的不同动力学区间。比较两种架构：非线性且表达能力强的Transformer，以及简单的线性预测模型DLinear。

Result: Transformer在更偏遍历的区间能准确预测动力学，但当初始态趋近疤痕极限时性能逐步退化。相反，DLinear在整个初始态族上保持准确，其主要偏差仅为小幅高频振荡，对整体预测误差影响很小。

Conclusion: 由复杂量子多体动力学产生的可观测量，可通过从过去观测到未来观测的简单线性映射实现高精度预测。这说明底层量子演化的复杂性并不必然转化为同等复杂的预测问题。

Abstract: Accurately simulating chaotic quantum many-body dynamics remains a major computational challenge for classical methods, due to the rapid buildup and spatial spreading of entanglement during the evolution. This raises the question of whether machine learning can provide an effective alternative for predicting quantum dynamics. We address this question by formulating quantum dynamics as a time-series forecasting problem, using a few-qubit PXP chain, realizable with Rydberg-atom arrays, as a benchmark. By varying the initial state, the system spans dynamical regimes ranging from ergodic behavior to quantum many-body scarring, providing a controlled setting for testing forecasting models across qualitatively different dynamics. We compare two contrasting architectures: an expressive nonlinear Transformer and DLinear, a simple linear forecasting model. The Transformer accurately predicts dynamics in the more ergodic regime, but its performance progressively deteriorates as the initial state approaches the scarred limit. In contrast, DLinear remains accurate across the entire family of initial states, with its main deviations consisting of small high-frequency oscillations that have little effect on the overall prediction error. Remarkably, these results show that observables generated by complex quantum many-body dynamics can be forecast with high accuracy through a simple linear mapping from past to future observations. This reveals that the complexity of the underlying quantum evolution need not translate into an equally complex forecasting problem.

</details>

*Devashish Tupkary,Zhiyao Wang,Ernest Y. -Z. Tan*

TL;DR: 本文为QKD中"单步哈希"协议（同时完成误码校验与隐私放大）提供数学安全性证明：在简单条件下，其安全性可由去掉最终误码校验步骤的略作修改的保密性概念推出；对多数基于熵的证明，密钥率不变，仅安全参数有微小变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统QKD需分别进行误码校验和隐私放大两步哈希，复杂度和资源开销大。单步哈希方案虽已出现于文献，但缺乏合适的数学论证，其安全性未被严格建立。

Method: 对哈希族输出，公开并比较固定数量的比特用于误码校验，其余比特作为最终密钥；在此构造下，证明在若干简单条件下，协议安全性可由"省略最终误码校验步骤"的略作修改的保密性定义推出。

Result: 对大多数基于熵的安全性证明，该合并协议可在密钥率不变的情况下实现，安全参数仅有微小变化。

Conclusion: 单步哈希QKD协议具有严格的安全性依据；它降低了经典后处理复杂度、经典通信量与所需随机性，且几乎不损失性能。

Abstract: Quantum Key Distribution (QKD) protocols typically involve two separate steps of error-verification and privacy amplification. Both steps involve the application of a hash function sampled from a suitable hash family to classical data. A potentially simpler alternative is to implement a single-step hashing procedure, announce and compare a fixed number of bits of its output for error-verification, and retain the remaining bits as the final output key. This reduces the complexity of classical postprocessing, the amount of classical communication, and the amount of randomness needed in the protocol. While this construction has appeared in the literature, a suitable mathematical justification is lacking. In this work, we show that the security of such protocols, under certain simple conditions, follows from a slightly modified notion of secrecy of the protocol without the final error-verification step. In particular, we show that for most entropy-based proofs, the combined protocol can be implemented with no change to the key rate, and a minor change to the security parameter.

</details>

Abstract: Distributed quantum computing requires rapid, high-fidelity entanglement between qubits in separate processor modules. Photonic links between single-atom qubits provide switchable, long-range connections, but photon loss limits their entanglement rate. Here we entangle a trapped $^{138}$Ba$^{+}$ ion with a silicon-vacancy (SiV$^{-}$) center in a diamond nanophotonic cavity. A photon emitted by the ion is converted in wavelength and encoding, reflected from the SiV$^{-}$--cavity system and detected, so that a single detected photon heralds entanglement. Including all conversion losses, we generate ion--SiV$^{-}$ Bell pairs at an average rate of 1.03(1)\,kHz with a fidelity of 87.9(7)\%. This is four times the rate of the fastest photonic link between two trapped ions and corresponds to one Bell pair per millisecond, matching the cycle time of planned trapped-ion processors.

Abstract: Quantum mechanics predicts that a vibrating object's energy comes in discrete packets, yet no measurement of its position reveals this discreteness. Resolving individual energy levels requires a qualitatively different measurement, one coupling to the resonator's energy rather than its displacement. We use a superconducting qubit dispersively coupled to a nanomechanical resonator to perform repeated quantum nondemolition measurements of the phonon number. An aligned transfer-print technique integrating the qubit and resonator yields a mechanical lifetime of $T_1 = 2.1$ milliseconds and a dispersive shift of $2χ/2π= 328$ kilohertz per phonon. We heralded single-phonon states with 85% fidelity and observed quantum jumps between the resonator's first excited state and ground state. These discontinuous transitions are a striking manifestation of quantum mechanics in a massive, vibrating object.

The accuracy of our estimates is ultimately limited by both computational and memory constraints; the former to reduce sampling error, the latter to avoid sampling bias. Traditional quantum algorithms -- such as amplitude estimation -- address only the former, and become untenable especially in near-future quantum devices where quantum memory is a premium. Our results thus advance the timeline for the practical deployment of quantum technologies, and illustrate their additional benefits when simulating processes of growing complexity.

A physical prediction is organized into three order-consistent steps: encoding into the retained description, evolution under the effective dynamics, and reconstruction of the physical observable. This viewpoint exposes failures caused by leakage, small denominators, neglected micromotion, inconsistent observables, memory effects, or incorrectly transformed dissipation, while linking derivation, physical interpretation, and mechanism-guided discovery. These results demonstrate that combining spectral partitioning, spatial circuit packing, and classical feasibility repair provides an executable path for deploying gate-based quantum sampling on thousand-variable constrained optimization problems on current hardware.

Abstract: We investigate the relaxation dynamics and steady state of a qubit interacting with a coherent environment through an energy-conserving flip-flop interaction within the Markovian repeated-interaction framework. In particular, coherent ancillas can increase the system ground- state population beyond that of the bath, corresponding to an effective temperature below the bath temperature, and enhance its steady-state purity, with these effects controlled by the interplay between the system energy scale and the system-ancilla coupling. Nevertheless, we prove that the purity of the system can never exceed the purity of the ancilla. Beyond calculation of the negativity, we analyze the associated eigenvalue spectra of partially transposed density matrices for qudit-qudit bipartitions, finding that higher negativity rank provides greater(reduced) robustness to targeted(depolarizing) noise. In particular, we extend the maximum number of separable states that can be arbitrarily mixed into an entangled pure ensemble while retaining negativity to be exactly equal to the original negativity rank. Abstract: Quantum fidelity estimation is a fundamental task in quantum information and computing to benchmark the quality of quantum states, and hence of quantum processes, on noisy quantum hardware. Point estimates of fidelity by existing methods generally lack the rigorous uncertainty quantification required for reliable preparation and verification of these tasks. While Bayesian and bootstrap frameworks are widely used for uncertainty quantification, they are sensitive to model misspecification or lack formal coverage guarantees in non-asymptotic regimes. This paper introduces a hybrid "Bayesian-informed" conformal prediction-based framework to provide distribution-free, rigorous prediction intervals for quantum state fidelity estimation which can be used, with care, to approximate confidence intervals with respect to the quantum randomness of Born's rule. Abstract: Quantum fault tolerance is a prerequisite to harness the full potential of quantum computers. However, achieving fault tolerance remains a major challenge since it requires optimizing tradeoffs between several intertwined theoretical and experimental parameters, including the encoding rate, code distance, parity-check weights, code planarity, and routing. We also address the routing problem of hyperbolic surface codes by introducing a topology-aware algorithm that partitions a hyperbolic code into bounded planar modules while reducing long-range communications demand. Circuit-level Monte Carlo simulations yield finite-size threshold estimates for both modular and monolithic layouts, showing that tripling the long-range gate error probability mildly lowers, but does not eliminate, the estimated error-correction thresholds. Together, these results strengthen the case for hyperbolic surface codes as structured and highly efficient large-scale quantum error correction codes for future fault-tolerant architectures.

Abstract: Spontaneous collapse models provide a possible, testable solution to the quantum measurement problem, but constructing a relativistic version applicable to quantum field theory remains an important open challenge. A modified, history-dependent Aharonov--Albert prescription describes instantaneous nonlocal state updates on arbitrary spacelike foliations while preserving foliation independence. The resulting dynamical map is linear and trace-preserving but not positive on the full set of standard density operators. Abstract: While conventional quantum resource theories quantify utility relative to an idealized set of free states, practical implementations are confined to a specific supply environment---a framework encompassing all states realizable or preparable via current experimental apparatus and design choices, not limited to free states. This work proposes a novel framework for evaluating the relative utility of arbitrary quantum states against the resource set made accessible by these current selections. We specifically focus our investigation on two canonical representations: the set of states reachable via free operations as the attainable boundary imposed by current experimental constraints, and the set defined through numerical optimization as the criterion for operational equivalence. Our framework establishes two core evaluation methodologies. First, we provide a strategy-dependent benchmark by assessing a state's performance relative to the reference set under its individually optimized strategy, thereby quantifying achievable performance. Second, we derive a computable lower bound on this superiority by considering optimal strategies for each state individually, enabling the quantification of strategy-independent relative performance independent of specific experimental setups. Beyond relative efficiency metrics, we demonstrate the practical utility of this approach for resource management scenarios, including the selective extraction of states advantageous for storage and distribution, and the concentration of residual resources. Using quantum entanglement as a paradigmatic example, we prove that distillable entanglement can be fully recovered via extraction and concentration when the reference set is restricted to pure states, thereby demonstrating the practical relevance of our framework.

Abstract: Quantum compilers play a critical role in transforming high-level quantum programs into optimized, hardware-compatible circuits. However, verifying the correctness of compiler passes remains challenging, as determining the expected output of large, deeply entangled quantum circuits is computationally intractable. This challenge is further amplified when compiler passes modify already complex circuit structures, making manual validation of transformed circuits impractical.

Our findings indicate validation is dominated by program-content and program-metric assertions, and test circuits are generally small and shallow. Motivated by these observations, we introduce a testing methodology for automated validation of quantum compiler passes based on retromorphic testing and principles from the Hadamard test. This methodology analyzes a compiler pass, test circuit, and expected pass behavior to verify semantic preservation and intended structural modifications.

Experimental evaluation reproduced several existing bugs as well as uncovered previously undetected defects, such as flawed symbolic parameter handling, incorrect commutation logic, failure to recognize self-adjointness of gates, and runtime crashes. These findings highlight the need for compiler-pass-specific testing methodologies to improve the reliability of the evolving quantum software stack.

Abstract: Qudits provide a larger local Hilbert space than qubits and have recently attracted interest as building blocks for efficient fault-tolerant quantum computation (FTQC). In this work, we extend many-hypercube (MHC) codes, high-rate concatenated quantum codes, from qubits to qudits of prime dimension $q$. We construct the level-2 and level-3 MHC codes with parameters $\left[\!\left[6^2,4^2,2^2\right]\!\right]_q$ and $\left[\!\left[6^3,4^3,2^3\right]\!\right]_q$, respectively, and generalize the level-by-level minimum-distance (LLMD) decoder originally proposed for the qubit MHC codes. In a qudit $X$-error model, we evaluate the code-capacity performance of the qudit MHC codes for dimensions up to $q=13$. We find that the error threshold increases monotonically with the local dimension $q$, reaching $10.0\%$ for $q=13$, nearly double the value of $5.1\%$ for qubits. Furthermore, for sufficiently large local dimensions, we observe a pronounced waterfall regime in which the logical error rate decreases substantially faster than expected from conventional distance-based estimates. To understand the origin of the observed qudit advantage, we analyze the decoding performance in terms of the entropy of the underlying $q$-ary noise channel. This analysis reveals a competition between the increased syndrome information available at larger $q$ and the simultaneous growth of the typical physical error weight at fixed entropy. These results demonstrate that higher-dimensional quantum systems can substantially improve the performance of high-rate concatenated quantum codes and motivate further development of qudit-based FTQC architectures.

Abstract: We study a hybrid system of coupled cavities, with or without two-level atoms doped inside. By employing a building-block construction facilitated by nonlinear Stark coupling, we identify a manifold of exact many-body eigenstates that remain analytically tractable across arbitrary lattice geometries and atom distributions. We demonstrate that these exact states exhibit a dual physical nature depending on their spectral positioning: when embedded within the chaotic many-body spectrum, they manifest as quantum scars that violate the eigenstate thermalization hypothesis and undergo weak ergodicity breaking; conversely, as ground states within fixed-total-excitation sectors, they exhibit off-diagonal long-range order and form polariton condensates. Furthermore, numerical calculations of local excitation-number fluctuations suggest that negative Stark coupling shifts the Mott-insulator to superfluid transition toward weaker inter-cavity hopping. Our findings provide a versatile framework for engineering both coherent quantum phases and non-thermalizing states in diverse light-matter architectures.

Abstract: Variational quantum algorithms are often limited by barren plateaus: gradients vanish as circuit size and depth increase, making quantum neural networks difficult to train. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在约束保持的量子退火/驱动设计中，存在大量保持同一可行扇区（feasible sector）的耦合图选择，它们在退火路径上表现出不同的最小间隙。如何从局部信息出发判断哪种驱动修改能有效提升间隙，是驱动优化的核心问题。

Result: 敏感度能可靠预测有益的无穷小改动，但当同一方向延伸为有限边替换时可靠性下降。间隙景观跟踪表明：改善当前活跃瓶颈会暴露一个竞争极小（低能子空间发生更大范围重组），即"修复-暴露"机制，并在稠密二次型与稀疏受阻（frustrated）目标族中均被观察到。在测试的oracle设置中，并列极小情形下的max-min更新显著优于沿原方向继续或仅优化进入极小。

Conclusion: 当竞争极小同时活跃时，需对响应进行重新线性化。局部交换分辨间隙敏感度可连接到瓶颈迁移与条件式驱动重定向，这种局部信息在超出无穷小极限后仍可能有用，但其实际提取依赖于驱动几何与相关低能竞争者的识别。

Abstract: Constraint-preserving exchange drivers allow many coupling graphs that preserve the same feasible sector, but exhibit different minimum gaps along an annealing path. We derive an exchange-resolved sensitivity of the normalised minimum gap for fixed-cardinality Laplacian drivers sharing a Dicke ground state, and apply it to identify locally favourable modifications of the driver. Numerical experiments indicate this sensitivity reliably predicts beneficial infinitesimal changes, but its reliability decreases when the same direction is extended to a finite edge replacement. The gap landscape tracking reveals one mechanism of this loss of reliability. Improving the active bottleneck can expose a competing minimum, with a more extensive reorganisation of the low-energy subspace. We call this the "repair-and-expose" mechanism and observe it in both dense quadratic and sparse frustrated objective families. This motivates relinearizing the response when competing minima become co-active.
